# Agents · E · part 8

729 entries, episode-prep to error-handling-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **episode-prep**: Checks a podcast episode transcript before anything is written from it: timestamps, speaker labels, name spellings and off-the-record parts, and warns if it is the raw recording rather than the published edit. Use for "prep this transcript", "check the transcript", "who is speaker 2", "repurpose this episode". ([jsath/claude-skills](https://github.com/jsath/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/video/podcast-repurpose/agents/01-episode-prep.md) · ★21)
- **episode-retro**: After an episode publishes — combines its YouTube stats with a film-study pass on the shipped master and writes the lessons as Ideas-desk entries tagged to the episode. The channel learns episode over episode. ([verillumai/the-ninth-room](https://github.com/verillumai/the-ninth-room/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/episode-retro.md) · ★1)
- **episode-reviewer**: Reviews one Autopilot episode (its trace summary, what the picture showed, the harness that played it) and proposes the one change for the next harness. Give it the run's folder (runs/&lt;id&gt;) and the episode's number; it reads with Autopilot's file tools and looks at frames where a death's cause is unclear. It can't act on the game or write. ([ramenvr/autopilot](https://github.com/ramenvr/autopilot/blob/HEAD/plugin/claude-code/agents/episode-reviewer.md) · ★876)
- **episode-rewriter**: 에피소드 재작성 실행 전문가. revision-analyst와 character-sculptor의 보고서를 통합하여 에피소드를 CLAUDE.md 집필 규칙에 따라 재작성. 자연스러운 한국어 문장 품질을 최우선으로 관리. ([MJbae/awesome-novel-studio](https://github.com/MJbae/awesome-novel-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/episode-rewriter.md) · ★161 · 4 copies)

- **epistemic**: 1. **Project Files & User Context** (HIGHEST): package.json, requirements.txt, go.mod, Cargo.toml, pyproject.toml are authoritative. User-provided facts = ground truth. Unknown feature/API = assume NEW, not error. 2. **External Tools & Documentation**: Web search, fetched docs, MCP responses override training data. 3. **Your Training Data** (LOWEST): "Legacy Archive" — reliable for syntax/logic, u ([mkoziy/ralpix](https://github.com/mkoziy/ralpix/blob/HEAD/bundled/agents/epistemic.md))
- **epistemic-analyst**: Use this agent when the user invokes /epistemic for multi-framework analysis — compares epistemic lenses, auto-suggests frameworks, or lets user pick interactively. Examples: ([askarzh/epistemator](https://github.com/askarzh/epistemator/blob/HEAD/agents/epistemic-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **epistemic-auditor**: Audits an output for the boundary between associative inference and confirmed claim. For each non-trivial factual claim about external state in the lead agent's output, decides whether it is paired with tool evidence (confirmed) or extrapolated (associative), and produces a list of items that must be marked inline before shipping. Use when the lead agent is about to send a reply that contains factual claims about code, repo state, config, or third-party systems. DOES NOT write implementation. ([AlexShchuka/neuro-matrix](https://github.com/AlexShchuka/neuro-matrix/blob/HEAD/agents/epistemic-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **epistemic-auditor**: Use this agent when the user asks for an independent check of the epistemic marks in an InSites stage output — whether each claim's mark (none, 〰️, 💭, citation) matches its actual basis in the uploaded sources. It reads with fresh context, flags, and never rewrites the output. &lt;example&gt; Context: Stage 2 of an InSites assessment has just been delivered. user: "audit this stage" assistant: "I'll send the Stage 2 output and the source files to the epistemic-auditor agent for an independent check." &lt;commentary&gt; The user asked for an audit; an agent that did not write the stage checks it, so the o… ([InSites-Lab/insites](https://github.com/InSites-Lab/insites/blob/HEAD/system/build/claude-plugin/agents/epistemic-auditor.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **epistemic-council**: Blind mode of the Council (skill council:council) — invoke at posture escalation (a pre-commit, high-cost-to-reverse decision) or as a completion verifier. Receives ONLY the reframed problem + positions WITHOUT authorship, never the thread's prose or the lean; runs ONE posture (bohr|schrodinger|epicurus|sagan|maxwell|zeno) and verifies by executing. The Council's only point of real structural separation. Output mirrors the thread's language, default English. Advisory. ([arthuravilacompass/agent-kit](https://github.com/arthuravilacompass/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/council/agents/epistemic-council.md))
- **epistemic-explore**: Research agent with enforced epistemic rigor. Classifies all findings as Verified, Inferred, or Guess with cited evidence. Use when investigating code, debugging, exploring unfamiliar systems, or when the user asks to research something. Can persist findings as pkm .ref.md artifacts when instructed. ([Nathan-Schwartz/dotfiles](https://github.com/Nathan-Schwartz/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/.claude/agents/epistemic-explore.md) · ★5)
- **epistemic-graphrag-specialist**: Use this agent when Orchestrate needs a read-only GraphRAG or GNN-RAG context brief from THG or other graph evidence surfaces. Typical triggers include choosing retrieval operators, mapping evidence paths, preserving graph traces, and identifying tensions or memory graph opportunities. Do not use it as the THG implementation owner or graph mutation agent. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([Travis-Gilbert/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Travis-Gilbert/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/theorems-harness/agents/epistemic-graphrag-specialist.md))
- **epistemic-labeler**: Applies explicit epistemic status labels to claims, catching the most common reasoning failure: stating conclusions with more confidence than the underlying evidence supports. ([newkayak12/claude-skills](https://github.com/newkayak12/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/cognition/skills/epistemic-reasoner/agents/epistemic-labeler.md) · ★3)

- **Equation-level tests for TIP / λ / belief coefficient; remove silent clamp**: The audit found: "Out of ~16 test files, exactly **one** asserts a thesis equation against a hand-computed value." W1-4 raises that count by adding equation-level tests for TIP (paper Eq 12), λ (paper Eq 13), and the belief coefficient (paper Eq 20) against known-analytical Gaussian cases. Plus an opt-out for the silent [0.05, 0.95] clamp so the disjoint test can see TIP near 0. ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W1-4-equation-level-tests.md) · ★1)
- **equation-verifier**: Use this agent to adversarially verify equation-digest rows against rendered PDFs or trusted publisher sources before they are marked implementation-ready. Typical triggers include "verify these equation rows", "check this digest against the PDF", "audit the Hurley equations", and any request to promote `needs-pdf-check` rows to `verified`. Do not use for ordinary literature summaries, code review after equations are implemented, or licensing decisions around reference code. ([drannarosen/research-workflow](https://github.com/drannarosen/research-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/equation-verifier.md) · ★1)
